Implement Phase 9 (Author test with golden runs) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 9).

LAUNCHER: every `megaplan` call = `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m megaplan ...`.

PHASE 9 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- `artagents author test --fixture <name>` runs an orchestrator's compiled plan against a fixture, auto-approves all decisions, and diffs the resulting `events.jsonl` against `<pack>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`.
- Fail on event drift, pass when the golden file matches (or is being intentionally regenerated).
- Files touched: `artagents/orchestrate/` (test verb), `artagents/core/task/` (auto-approval mode), `artagents/packs/*/fixtures/`, `artagents/packs/*/golden/`.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Fixture tests fail on event drift and pass when intentionally regenerated.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. Author-test CLI (in artagents/orchestrate/cli.py):
   - `artagents author test --pack <pack-id> --orchestrator <orch-id> --fixture <fixture-name>`
   - Loads the orchestrator's compiled plan from `<pack>/build/<orch>.json`.
   - Loads the fixture from `<pack>/fixtures/<fixture-name>/` — fixture provides initial inputs and any external artifacts the steps need.
   - Runs the plan in a temporary scratch directory with auto-approval ON for all attested steps (so it doesn't block).
   - After completion, normalizes the resulting `events.jsonl` (strip timestamps, run-ids, absolute paths — keep only structural fields).
   - Diffs against `<pack>/golden/<fixture-name>.events.jsonl`.
   - Returns 0 on match, 1 on drift with a unified diff printed.

2. Auto-approval mode for attested steps (in core/task/lifecycle_ack.py or new helper):
   - When `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` is set, attested steps that would normally block on user ack auto-approve and record a synthetic ack event marked `source: author_test`.
   - This MUST NOT be available in normal `start` flow — only `author test` sets the env var.

3. Event normalization helper (artagents/core/task/normalize.py):
   - Strip volatile fields: `timestamp`, `run_id`, `pid`, absolute paths (replace with `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>`).
   - Keep structural fields: `event_type`, `step_id`, `decision`, `produces` keys, `cas_hash`, etc.
   - Hash-chain hashes are also volatile (depend on stripped fields) — recompute over normalized form, OR strip them entirely. Pick one and document.

4. Regenerate workflow:
   - `artagents author test --fixture <name> --regenerate` writes the current events.jsonl as the new golden.
   - Print a confirmation message reminding the author to commit the regenerated golden if it's intentional.

5. Golden fixture for the canonical hype orchestrator:
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/` — minimal inputs (a small audio file or stub).
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/golden/smoke.events.jsonl` — the expected event sequence.

6. Tests:
   - `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`: run author-test against the hype/smoke fixture, assert it passes against the committed golden.
   - `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`: corrupt the golden in a temp copy, assert author-test reports drift with unified diff.
   - `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`: run with --regenerate, assert golden file is rewritten.
   - `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`: assert auto-approval only fires when ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1.

CONSTRAINTS:
- This is the LAST phase of the V1 design. Polish matters here.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies (stdlib only).
- The author-test must NOT touch real project state — runs in scratch dirs.
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state, per-project CAS.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 9 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new tests + `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` exits 0 against a committed golden file.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Phase 9 — Author Test with Golden Runs

## Overview

Phase 9 implements `artagents author test` as a full runtime replay: compile the orchestrator DSL, run the plan in a scratch project directory with auto-approval for all attested steps, normalize the resulting `events.jsonl` (strip volatile fields), and diff against the pack's golden fixture. The scaffolded `_cmd_test` in `artagents/orchestrate/cli.py` currently does file-vs-file diff only — this phase replaces it with real plan execution.

Key constraints from the design doc:
- Runs in scratch dirs — does not touch real project state
- Auto-approval mode for attested steps must NOT be available in normal `start`/`next` flow
- Hash-chain hashes are stripped during normalization (they depend on volatile fields)
- `--regenerate` flag writes current output as the new golden
- Needs a canonical `builtin.hype` fixture + golden file

## Phase 1: Foundation — Auto-Approval Mode + Normalization

### Step 1: Add auto-approval env constant and check (`artagents/core/task/env.py`)
**Scope:** Tiny

1. **Add** `AUTHOR_TEST_ENV = "ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST"` alongside existing constants (around line 13).
2. **Add** `def is_author_test() -> bool: return os.environ.get(AUTHOR_TEST_ENV) == "1"` helper.
3. **Export** both in `artagents/core/task/__init__.py`.

### Step 2: Add auto-approval path in `validate_attested_identity` (`artagents/core/task/gate.py`)
**Scope:** Small

1. **Insert** at the top of `validate_attested_identity` (before line 1071 args checks): when `is_author_test()` is True and `args.agent == "author-test"`, return `("agent", "author-test")` immediately — bypassing all identity validation (no ARTAGENTS_ACTOR check, no self-ack check).
2. Also allow `--agent author-test` when neither agent nor actor is passed: the `AttestedArgs` from `match_attested_command` will have `agent=None`, so the auto-approval path in `_dispatch_attested` must also check the env BEFORE calling `validate_attested_identity` and synthesize the args.

**Better approach** (simpler): In `_dispatch_attested` (line 904), BEFORE calling `match_attested_command`, check `is_author_test()`. If True, skip `match_attested_command` entirely, skip `validate_attested_identity`, and directly emit the attested event with `attestor_kind="agent"`, `attestor_id="author-test"`, evidence containing `"source:author_test"`. This avoids touching `validate_attested_identity` at all and keeps the bypass self-contained.

### Step 3: Create event normalization helper (`artagents/core/task/normalize.py`)
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Define** `VOLATILE_FIELDS = frozenset({"ts", "hash", "run_id", "pid"})`.
2. **Define** `def normalize_event(event: dict) -> dict`: deep-copy, strip volatile fields, replace absolute paths matching `<scratch_root>/...` with `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>`.
3. **Define** `def normalize_events_jsonl(lines: list[str], scratch_root: str) -> list[str]`: parse each line, normalize, re-serialize with `sort_keys=True`.
4. Strategy for hash-chain hashes: **strip them entirely**. Since they depend on `ts` and `prev_hash`, they cannot be meaningfully compared across runs. The structural fields (`kind`, `plan_step_id`, `command`, `returncode`, `produces_name`, `check_id`, `attestor_kind`, `attestor_id`) are the comparison surface.

### Step 4: Add `--regenerate` flag to CLI parser (`artagents/orchestrate/cli.py`)
**Scope:** Tiny

1. **Add** `test_p.add_argument("--regenerate", action="store_true", help="...")` around line 471.
2. **Update** `main()` to pass `args.regenerate` through to `_cmd_test`.

## Phase 2: Core — Author Test Runtime Replay

### Step 5: Implement the real `_cmd_test` runtime replay (`artagents/orchestrate/cli.py`)
**Scope:** Large — replaces the scaffold `_cmd_test` (lines 311-371)

1. **Compile** the orchestrator DSL to get `plan.json`:
   - `compile_to_path(qid, packs_root=packs_root)` writes to `<pack>/build/<name>.json`
   - Load it: `plan = load_plan(build_path)`
   - Compute hash: `plan_hash = compute_plan_hash(build_path)`

2. **Create scratch project directory**:
   - `scratch = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="artagents-author-test-"))`
   - `project_dir = scratch / fixture_name` (the project slug)
   - Copy `plan.json` from build
   - Write `active_run.json` with `{"run_id": "test-run", "plan_hash": plan_hash}`
   - Create `runs/test-run/` dir
   - Set `ARTAGENTS_PROJECTS_ROOT` to `scratch`
   - Set `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1`

3. **Drive the plan step-by-step**:
   - For each step in plan order (handling nested/repeat traversal):
     - Call `gate_command(slug=fixture_name, command=step.command, argv=[], root=scratch)`
     - For `CodeStep`: run `subprocess.run(shlex.split(step.command), env=child_subprocess_env(), cwd=step_dir, check=False)`, then `record_dispatch_complete(decision, returncode)`
     - For `AttestedStep`: the auto-approval in `_dispatch_attested` fires; after gate returns, call `record_dispatch_complete(decision, 0)` (which is no-op for attested per FLAG-007)
     - For `NestedStep`: the gate's `_auto_traverse_to_leaf` handles nested_entered; after child steps complete, gate does nested_exited
   - Stop when `gate_command` raises `TaskRunGateError` with "plan is exhausted"

4. **After completion**:
   - Read `events.jsonl` from `scratch/fixture_name/runs/test-run/`
   - Normalize with `normalize_events_jsonl()`
   - If `--regenerate`: write normalized output to `<pack>/golden/<fixture_name>.events.jsonl`, print confirmation, exit 0
   - Else: load golden, normalize it too, diff with `difflib.unified_diff`, exit 0 on match / 1 on drift

5. **Cleanup**: `shutil.rmtree(scratch, ignore_errors=True)` in a `finally` block.

### Step 6: Create the hype DSL orchestrator file (`artagents/packs/builtin/hype/hype.py`)
**Scope:** Medium

Following the canonical migration example from section 13 of the design doc:
```python
from artagents.orchestrate import code, nested, plan
from artagents.verify import file_nonempty, json_file

hype = plan("builtin.hype", [
    code("transcribe", argv=["python3", "-m", "artagents", "executors", "run", "builtin.transcribe", "..."], produces={"transcript": json_file("transcript.json")}),
    code("cut", argv=["python3", "-m", "artagents", "executors", "run", "builtin.cut", "..."], produces={"timeline": json_file("hype.timeline.json"), "assets": json_file("hype.assets.json")}),
    code("render", argv=["python3", "-m", "artagents", "executors", "run", "builtin.render", "..."], produces={"video": file_nonempty("hype.mp4")}),
    nested("thumbnail", plan="builtin.thumbnail_maker", produces={"thumbnail": file_nonempty("thumbnail.png")}),
])
```

BUT — for the smoke fixture, we want a MINIMAL plan that does NOT invoke real executors (which would need real inputs, API keys, etc.). The smoke fixture's orchestrator should use trivial shell commands:
```python
from artagents.orchestrate import code, plan
from artagents.verify import file_nonempty

hype = plan("builtin.hype", [
    code("smoke-1", argv=["echo", "hello"], produces={"out": file_nonempty("out.txt")}),
    code("smoke-2", argv=["echo", "world"], produces={"out2": file_nonempty("out2.txt")}),
])
```

Wait — the design doc says the canonical hype orchestrator should use the real executor pipeline. But for `author test`, we need a plan that runs deterministically without external dependencies. The answer: create the REAL `hype.py` per the design doc, but have the **smoke fixture** use a separate test orchestrator (`builtin.hype-smoke`?) or use the real one but with a fixture that provides stub inputs.

**Decision**: Create `hype.py` as the design doc specifies. For the smoke test, create a separate minimal orchestrator `hype_smoke.py` or put both in `hype.py`. The simplest: put the smoke plan as a second plan in `hype.py` named `builtin.hype-smoke`:

```python
from artagents.orchestrate import code, nested, plan
from artagents.verify import file_nonempty, json_file

hype = plan("builtin.hype", [
    code("transcribe", argv=[...], produces={"transcript": json_file("transcript.json")}),
    ...
])

hype_smoke = plan("builtin.hype-smoke", [
    code("step-1", argv=["echo", "ok"], produces={"out": file_nonempty("out.txt")}),
])
```

Actually, re-reading the design: the design says *canonical migration* uses `hype.py`. The smoke fixture is for testing the author-test mechanism itself. Let's do this cleanly:

1. Create `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/hype.py` with the real plan
2. The smoke fixture test will use a DIFFERENT orchestrator created in the test fixture (just like `test_author_cli.py` does with `_VALID_FOO`)
3. Create `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/` with minimal inputs
4. Create `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/golden/smoke.events.jsonl` as a committed golden

For the committed golden to work, the smoke fixture must use deterministic commands. We'll create a small smoke orchestrator in the test setup OR create it as a committed file. The cleanest: create `hype.py` with both the real plan and a smoke plan, then the golden is generated from the smoke plan.

**Final approach**:
- `hype.py` contains the real `builtin.hype` plan (per design doc) AND a `builtin.hype-smoke` plan that uses trivial echo commands
- `fixtures/smoke/` contains an empty `.keep` file (no special inputs needed for echo commands)
- `golden/smoke.events.jsonl` is committed — it's the expected event sequence from running `builtin.hype-smoke`

## Phase 3: Tests

### Step 7: Create `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Set up** a test pack with a minimal orchestrator (2 code steps using echo)
2. **Pre-generate** the golden file by running the test flow and capturing
3. **Run** `author_cli.main(["test", "pack.name", "--fixture", "f1"], packs_root=packs)`
4. **Assert** exit code 0, output contains "ok"

### Step 8: Create `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`
**Scope:** Small

1. **Set up** same as above
2. **Corrupt** the golden by changing an event's `kind`
3. **Run** author test
4. **Assert** exit code 1, stdout contains unified diff with the changed field

### Step 9: Create `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`
**Scope:** Small

1. **Set up** same, but delete the golden file first
2. **Run** `author_cli.main(["test", ..., "--regenerate"], ...)`
3. **Assert** exit code 0, golden file was created with expected content
4. **Run** author test again without --regenerate, assert it passes against the newly-written golden

### Step 10: Create `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Set up** a plan with an attested step
2. **Run** through the author-test flow, assert the attested step is auto-approved
3. **Verify** the emitted event has `attestor_kind: "agent"`, `attestor_id: "author-test"`
4. **Verify** that without `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1`, the same attested step call rejects (missing --agent)
5. **Verify** that the auto-approval env check is NOT available through `artagents next` (test that `is_author_test()` returns False when env is not set)

### Step 11: Update existing test `tests/test_author_test_scaffold.py`
**Scope:** Small

1. The existing scaffold tests check for exit code 2 + "Phase 9" message when golden is missing. After Phase 9, the behavior changes: missing golden is still an error, but now the real runtime runs and produces an events.jsonl, then reports "no golden to compare against". Update the test expectations to match the new behavior.

2. Alternatively, keep the scaffold test but rename/repurpose it — the `missing_golden` test now expects exit code 1 with a "no golden file" message instead of exit code 2 with "implement Phase 9".

## Execution Order

1. Step 1 (env constant) — no dependencies
2. Step 2 (auto-approval in gate) — depends on Step 1
3. Step 3 (normalization) — no dependencies, but needed by Step 5
4. Step 4 (--regenerate flag) — depends on nothing
5. Step 5 (runtime replay in _cmd_test) — depends on Steps 1-4
6. Step 6 (hype.py DSL + fixture/golden) — depends on Step 5 to generate golden
7. Steps 7-10 (tests) — depend on Steps 1-6
8. Step 11 (update scaffold tests) — depends on Step 5

## Validation Order

1. `python3 -m pytest tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py -v` — verify auto-approval mechanism
2. `python3 -m pytest tests/test_author_test_pass.py -v` — verify happy path
3. `python3 -m pytest tests/test_author_test_drift.py -v` — verify drift detection
4. `python3 -m pytest tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py -v` — verify regenerate
5. `python3 -m pytest tests/test_author_test_scaffold.py -v` — verify updated scaffold
6. `python3 -m pytest tests/ -v` — full suite, verify no regressions
7. Manual: `python3 -m artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype-smoke --fixture smoke` — exits 0

Tests:\n   - `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`: run author-test against the hype/smoke fixture, assert it passes against the committed golden.\n   - `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`: corrupt the golden in a temp copy, assert author-test reports drift with unified diff.\n   - `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`: run with --regenerate, assert golden file is rewritten.\n   - `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`: assert auto-approval only fires when ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1.\n\nCONSTRAINTS:\n- This is the LAST phase of the V1 design. Polish matters here.\n- Additive only.         Robustness level:
        standard

        Requirements:
        - Decide exactly one of: PROCEED, ITERATE, ESCALATE, TIEBREAKER.
        - Use the weighted score, flag details (including `evidence`), plan delta, recurring critiques, and preflight results as judgment context.
        - PROCEED when execution should move forward now.
        - ITERATE when revising the plan is the best next move.
        - ESCALATE when the loop is stuck, churn is recurring, or user intervention is needed.
        - TIEBREAKER when a flag group reflects an *unresolvable constraint tension* (architectural or philosophical — requires a human call) rather than a plan-quality issue. Use TIEBREAKER only when the Iteration Pressure Analysis shows `addressed_then_reopened_count >= 2` for a fuzzy group OR the group has >=2 member flags across >=2 iterations. If the concern is simply that the plan writer hasn't tried hard enough, use ITERATE instead. When recommending TIEBREAKER you MUST provide `tiebreaker_question` (the decision question for human resolution), `tiebreaker_flag_ids` (which flags this resolves), and `tiebreaker_fuzzy_group_id` (which group this resolves). Cite specific flag IDs and iterations in your rationale.
        - `signals_assessment`: one paragraph summarizing score trajectory, flag status, and preflight posture.

        Flags come in two tiers:
        - **Blocking** (severity = significant/likely-significant): These are serious concerns. If you recommend PROCEED, you MUST provide a `flag_resolutions` entry for every blocking flag. There is no implicit acceptance.
        - **Noted** (everything else): Acknowledge in your rationale but they don't block PROCEED.

        If there are blocking flags and you want to PROCEED, provide `flag_resolutions` with one entry per blocking flag. If you cannot resolve every blocking flag, choose ITERATE (send back for revision) or ESCALATE (human intervention needed).
        Structurally unresolvable flags (for example, infrastructure outside the repo or product decisions that require a human) are ESCALATE, not PROCEED with a non-answer.

        For each blocking flag:
        - **dispute**: The critique is factually wrong. Evidence must cite something specific (file path, line, API doc, etc.). Generic statements like "handled correctly" are invalid.
        - **accept_tradeoff**: The concern is real but intentionally accepted as a known limitation. Rationale must be specific to this flag. Boilerplate like "acceptable within scope" is invalid.
        - Schema requirement: every `flag_resolutions` entry must include both `evidence` and `rationale`. Use `""` for the field that does not apply to that action.

        If there are no blocking flags, return `flag_resolutions: []`.
        Always return `accepted_tradeoffs`; use `[]` when none apply.

        Populate `settled_decisions` with design choices that should carry into review without re-litigation. Return `[]` when there are none.

        Example:
        ```json
        {
          "recommendation": "PROCEED",
          "rationale": "Core fix is correct. Convention concern accepted.",
          "signals_assessment": "Score stable at 2.5, preflight passed, no recurring critiques.",
          "warnings": ["Verify edge case with composite moduli during execution."],
          "flag_resolutions": [
            {"flag_id": "correctness-1", "action": "dispute", "evidence": "allow_migrate and allow_migrate_model produce identical behavior for this use case (verified at django/db/utils.py:286).", "rationale": ""},
            {"flag_id": "performance-1", "action": "accept_tradeoff", "evidence": "", "rationale": "Cold-start latency remains 40ms above target because the cache warmup job is owned by platform and outside this repo; rollout is still approved for the limited internal beta."},
            {"flag_id": "conventions-1", "action": "accept_tradeoff", "evidence": "", "rationale": "Minor naming inconsistency is confined to this helper and would create churn across generated fixtures; track it as follow-up cleanup instead of blocking this fix."}
          ],
          "accepted_tradeoffs": [],
          "settled_decisions": []
        }
        ```

Implement Phase 9 (Author test with golden runs) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 9).

PHASE 9 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- `artagents author test --fixture <name>` runs an orchestrator's compiled plan against a fixture, auto-approves all decisions, and diffs the resulting `events.jsonl` against `<pack>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`.
- Fail on event drift, pass when the golden file matches (or is being intentionally regenerated).
- Files touched: `artagents/orchestrate/` (test verb), `artagents/core/task/` (auto-approval mode), `artagents/packs/*/fixtures/`, `artagents/packs/*/golden/`.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Fixture tests fail on event drift and pass when intentionally regenerated.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. Author-test CLI (in artagents/orchestrate/cli.py):
   - `artagents author test --pack <pack-id> --orchestrator <orch-id> --fixture <fixture-name>`
   - Loads the orchestrator's compiled plan from `<pack>/build/<orch>.json`.
   - Loads the fixture from `<pack>/fixtures/<fixture-name>/` — fixture provides initial inputs and any external artifacts the steps need.
   - Runs the plan in a temporary scratch directory with auto-approval ON for all attested steps (so it doesn't block).
   - After completion, normalizes the resulting `events.jsonl` (strip timestamps, run-ids, absolute paths — keep only structural fields).
   - Diffs against `<pack>/golden/<fixture-name>.events.jsonl`.
   - Returns 0 on match, 1 on drift with a unified diff printed.

2. Auto-approval mode for attested steps (in core/task/lifecycle_ack.py or new helper):
   - When `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` is set, attested steps that would normally block on user ack auto-approve and record a synthetic ack event marked `source: author_test`.
   - This MUST NOT be available in normal `start` flow — only `author test` sets the env var.

3. Event normalization helper (artagents/core/task/normalize.py):
   - Strip volatile fields: `timestamp`, `run_id`, `pid`, absolute paths (replace with `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>`).
   - Keep structural fields: `event_type`, `step_id`, `decision`, `produces` keys, `cas_hash`, etc.
   - Hash-chain hashes are also volatile (depend on stripped fields) — recompute over normalized form, OR strip them entirely. Pick one and document.

4. Regenerate workflow:
   - `artagents author test --fixture <name> --regenerate` writes the current events.jsonl as the new golden.
   - Print a confirmation message reminding the author to commit the regenerated golden if it's intentional.

5. Golden fixture for the canonical hype orchestrator:
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/` — minimal inputs (a small audio file or stub).
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/golden/smoke.events.jsonl` — the expected event sequence.

6. Tests:
   - `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`: run author-test against the hype/smoke fixture, assert it passes against the committed golden.
   - `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`: corrupt the golden in a temp copy, assert author-test reports drift with unified diff.
   - `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`: run with --regenerate, assert golden file is rewritten.
   - `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`: assert auto-approval only fires when ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1.

CONSTRAINTS:
- This is the LAST phase of the V1 design. Polish matters here.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies (stdlib only).
- The author-test must NOT touch real project state — runs in scratch dirs.
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state, per-project CAS.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 9 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new tests + `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` exits 0 against a committed golden file.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Phase 9 — Author Test with Golden Runs (Revised)

## Overview

Phase 9 implements `artagents author test` as a full runtime replay that mirrors the real `start`/`next` lifecycle: initialize a project directory in a scratch location, drive the plan step-by-step by calling `gate_command` in a loop, auto-approve attested steps via `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1`, normalize the resulting `events.jsonl`, and diff against the pack's committed golden file. The scaffolded `_cmd_test` in `artagents/orchestrate/cli.py` (lines 311–371) is replaced, and the inlined `_strip_volatile` helper is consolidated into the new `normalize.py` module.

Key constraints:
- Runs in scratch dirs via `tempfile.mkdtemp` — does not touch real project state.
- Auto-approval for attested steps gated behind `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1`; NOT available in normal `start`/`next`.
- Hash-chain hashes (`hash`) and timestamps (`ts`) are stripped during normalization; `run_id`, `actor`, and absolute paths are also stripped or normalized to `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>`.
- `--regenerate` flag writes current output as the new golden.
- CLI surface matches the stop condition: `--pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke`.
- Existing `test_author_test_scaffold.py` tests are rewritten to match the new runtime-replay behavior.

## Phase 1: Foundation — Env Constant, Normalization, CLI Parser

### Step 1: Add auto-approval env constant (`artagents/core/task/env.py` + `__init__.py`)
**Scope:** Tiny

1. **Add** `AUTHOR_TEST_ENV = "ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST"` after line 13, alongside `ARTAGENTS_ACTOR`.
2. **Add** `def is_author_test() -> bool: return os.environ.get(AUTHOR_TEST_ENV) == "1"`.
3. **Export** both in `artagents/core/task/__init__.py`: add `AUTHOR_TEST_ENV` and `is_author_test` to imports from `.env` and to `__all__`.

### Step 2: Create event normalization module (`artagents/core/task/normalize.py`)
**Scope:** Medium — consolidates and replaces the inlined `_strip_volatile` at `cli.py:287-308`

1. **Define** `VOLATILE_FIELDS = frozenset({"ts", "hash", "run_id", "actor"})`.
   - `ts` and `hash` are inherently volatile (timestamps, hash-chain dependent).
   - `run_id` is tied to the generated run name — different every time.
   - `actor` comes from `ARTAGENTS_ACTOR` / hostname — would drift across machines/CI.
2. **Define** `def normalize_event(event: dict, *, scratch_root: str) -> dict`:
   - Deep-copy the event dict.
   - Strip `VOLATILE_FIELDS` keys.
   - Replace absolute paths matching `<scratch_root>/...` with `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>` in any string value (case-insensitive, to handle the project directory path).
   - Return the cleaned dict.
3. **Define** `def normalize_events_jsonl(lines: list[str], scratch_root: str) -> list[str]`:
   - Parse each line as JSON, call `normalize_event`, re-serialize with `sort_keys=True`.
   - Skip empty lines. On JSON decode failure, keep the raw line (same as current `_strip_volatile` behavior).
4. **Export** `normalize_event` and `normalize_events_jsonl` in `artagents/core/task/__init__.py`.
5. **Replace** uses of `_strip_volatile` in `cli.py` with `normalize_events_jsonl` (see Step 5).

### Step 3: Rewrite CLI parser to accept `--pack` and `--orchestrator` flags (`artagents/orchestrate/cli.py`)
**Scope:** Medium — replaces the single `qualified_id` positional for `test`

1. **In `_build_parser()`** (around line 468):
   - Replace the single `qualified_id` positional on the `test` subparser with three named arguments:
     ```python
     test_p = sub.add_parser("test", help="author test --pack <pack> --orchestrator <name> --fixture <name>")
     test_p.add_argument("--pack", required=True, help="pack id (e.g., builtin)")
     test_p.add_argument("--orchestrator", required=True, help="orchestrator name (e.g., hype)")
     test_p.add_argument("--fixture", required=True, help="fixture name (under <pack>/fixtures/)")
     test_p.add_argument("--regenerate", action="store_true", help="overwrite golden with current events")
     ```
   - This satisfies the stop-condition invocation: `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke`.
2. **In `main()`** (line 491): update the `test` dispatch to construct `qid = f"{args.pack}.{args.orchestrator}"` and pass `args.regenerate` through:
   ```python
   if args.cmd == "test":
       qid = f"{args.pack}.{args.orchestrator}"
       return _cmd_test(qid, args.fixture, packs_root, regenerate=args.regenerate)
   ```
3. **Update `_cmd_test` signature** to accept `regenerate: bool = False`.

## Phase 2: Core — Runtime Replay + Auto-Approval

### Step 4: Add auto-approval path in `_dispatch_attested` (`artagents/core/task/gate.py`)
**Scope:** Small — insert at the top of `_dispatch_attested` (before line 918)

1. **Insert** at the top of `_dispatch_attested`, right after the docstring and before `match_attested_command`:
   ```python
   if is_author_test():
       append_event(
           events_path,
           make_step_attested_event(path_str, "agent", "author-test", ("source=author_test",)),
       )
       decision = GateDecision(
           active=True,
           run_id=run_id,
           plan_step_id=path_str,
           events_path=events_path,
           reentry=False,
           step_kind="attested",
           slug=slug,
           plan_step_path=path_tuple,
           produces=step.produces,
           project_root=project_root,
           iteration=iteration,
           item_id=item_id,
       )
       if step.produces:
           _run_inline_checks(decision, step.produces)
       return decision
   ```
   - This bypasses all identity validation (`match_attested_command`, `validate_attested_identity`) when `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1`.
   - The emitted event has `attestor_kind="agent"`, `attestor_id="author-test"`, and evidence `["source=author_test"]`.
   - Inline produces checks still run (important for rewind-on-fail fidelity).

### Step 5: Implement the real `_cmd_test` runtime replay (`artagents/orchestrate/cli.py`)
**Scope:** Large — replaces the scaffold `_cmd_test` (lines 311–371)

1. **Compile** the orchestrator DSL to get the plan:
   - Call `compile_to_path(qid, packs_root=packs_root)` to ensure `<pack>/build/<name>.json` exists.
   - Load it: `plan = load_plan(build_path)`.

2. **Create scratch project directory**:
   - `scratch = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="artagents-author-test-"))`
   - `project_dir = scratch / fixture_name` (the project slug)
   - Write `plan.json` from the compiled build payload into `project_dir / "plan.json"`.
   - Compute `plan_hash = compute_plan_hash(project_dir / "plan.json")`.
   - Generate a deterministic run_id: `run_id = "test-run"`.
   - Write `active_run.json`: `{"run_id": run_id, "plan_hash": plan_hash}`.
   - Create `project_dir / "runs" / run_id /`.
   - Append `run_started` event to `events.jsonl` (with `actor=None` to avoid hostname drift).

3. **Save and set env vars** (with restore in finally):
   ```python
   saved_project_root = os.environ.get("ARTAGENTS_PROJECTS_ROOT")
   saved_author_test = os.environ.get(AUTHOR_TEST_ENV)
   os.environ["ARTAGENTS_PROJECTS_ROOT"] = str(scratch)
   os.environ[AUTHOR_TEST_ENV] = "1"
   ```

4. **Drive the plan step-by-step in a loop**:
   - Load `plan.json` and iterate through `plan.steps` (handling each step in linear order; `gate_command`'s internal `_auto_traverse_to_leaf` handles nested/repeat).
   - For each step:
     - Call `gate_command(slug=fixture_name, command=step.command, argv=[], root=scratch)`.
     - If `decision.step_kind == "code"`:
       - Run `subprocess.run(shlex.split(step.command), env=child_subprocess_env(), cwd=step_dir_for_path(...), check=False)`.
       - Call `record_dispatch_complete(decision, returncode)`.
     - If `decision.step_kind == "attested"`:
       - Auto-approval already fired inside `gate_command`. `record_dispatch_complete` is a no-op for attested (line 1099-1101).
     - Continue loop to next step.
   - Stop when `gate_command` raises `TaskRunGateError` with "plan is exhausted" (caught and treated as successful completion).

5. **After completion** (or on any error):
   - Read `events.jsonl` from `scratch/fixture_name/runs/test-run/`.
   - Normalize with `normalize_events_jsonl(events_lines, str(scratch))`.
   - If `regenerate`: write normalized output to `<pack>/golden/<fixture_name>.events.jsonl`, print confirmation ("golden regenerated: <path>; commit this file if intentional"), exit 0.
   - Else:
     - Load golden file from `<pack>/golden/<fixture_name>.events.jsonl`.
     - If golden missing: print "no golden file at <path>; use --regenerate to create one", exit 1.
     - Normalize golden with same normalization (golden may already be pre-normalized but we normalize for safety).
     - Diff with `difflib.unified_diff`. Exit 0 on match, exit 1 on drift (printing unified diff to stdout).

6. **Cleanup**: In a `finally` block:
   - Restore env vars: `os.environ["ARTAGENTS_PROJECTS_ROOT"] = saved_project_root` (or `os.environ.pop` if none). Same for `AUTHOR_TEST_ENV`.
   - `shutil.rmtree(scratch, ignore_errors=True)`.

7. **Remove** the old `_strip_volatile` function (lines 287–308) and `_VOLATILE_EVENT_FIELDS` constant — they are now consolidated in `normalize.py`.

## Phase 3: Hype Pack — DSL, Fixture, Golden

### Step 6: Create the hype orchestrator pack (`artagents/packs/builtin/hype/`)
**Scope:** Medium — new pack directory with DSL, fixtures, golden

1. **Create `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/hype.py`** with the canonical `builtin.hype` plan:
   ```python
   from artagents.orchestrate import code, nested, plan
   from artagents.verify import file_nonempty, json_file

   hype = plan("builtin.hype", [
       code("transcribe", argv=[
           "python3", "-m", "artagents", "executors", "run", "builtin.transcribe",
           "--audio", "<FIXTURE_AUDIO>",
       ], produces={"transcript": json_file("transcript.json")}),
       code("cut", argv=[
           "python3", "-m", "artagents", "executors", "run", "builtin.cut",
           "--brief", "<FIXTURE_BRIEF>",
       ], produces={"timeline": json_file("hype.timeline.json"), "assets": json_file("hype.assets.json")}),
       code("render", argv=[
           "python3", "-m", "artagents", "executors", "run", "builtin.render",
       ], produces={"video": file_nonempty("hype.mp4")}),
       nested("thumbnail", plan="builtin.thumbnail_maker", produces={"thumbnail": file_nonempty("thumbnail.png")}),
   ])
   ```
   Note: `<FIXTURE_AUDIO>` and `<FIXTURE_BRIEF>` are placeholders — at runtime, the fixture provides real paths. But since author-test runs against this plan in a scratch dir with auto-approval, the code steps would actually try to run the real executors, which need API keys. **For the smoke fixture test to pass deterministically**, we use a smoke-only plan.

2. **Create `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/hype_smoke.py`** with deterministic echo commands:
   ```python
   from artagents.orchestrate import attested, code, plan
   from artagents.verify import file_nonempty

   hype_smoke = plan("builtin.hype-smoke", [
       code("step-1", argv=["echo", "hello"], produces={"out": file_nonempty("out.txt")}),
       code("step-2", argv=["echo", "world"], produces={"out2": file_nonempty("out2.txt")}),
       attested("review", command="review.sh", instructions="peer review", ack="agent"),
   ])
   ```
   - Three steps: two code steps (echo), one attested step.
   - This plan is fully deterministic and requires no external dependencies.

3. **Create `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/`**:
   - Write a `.keep` file (no special inputs needed for echo commands).

4. **Create `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/golden/smoke.events.jsonl`** — the committed golden:
   - This will be generated by running `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype-smoke --fixture smoke --regenerate` once the runtime replay is working.
   - Expected structure: `run_started`, `step_dispatched` (step-1), `step_completed` (step-1), `step_dispatched` (step-2), `step_completed` (step-2), `step_attested` (review with attestor_id=author-test).

### Step 7: Run the smoke test to generate the committed golden (build-time step)
**Scope:** Operational — executed once during implementation to create the committed golden

1. **Run**: `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m artagents author compile --pack builtin --orchestrator hype-smoke`.
2. **Run**: `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype-smoke --fixture smoke --regenerate`.
3. **Verify** the generated `golden/smoke.events.jsonl` contains the expected 6 events.
4. **Commit** the golden file.

## Phase 4: Tests

### Step 8: Rewrite `tests/test_author_test_scaffold.py`
**Scope:** Medium — the existing scaffold tests test Phase 5 behavior; Phase 9 replaces that

1. **`test_missing_golden_returns_2_with_phase9_message`** → rename to `test_missing_golden_returns_1_with_regenerate_hint`:
   - Set up a pack with the smoke orchestrator, build it, create scratch project dir, create fixture dir with `.keep`.
   - Run author test (no golden file exists).
   - Assert exit code 1 (not 2).
   - Assert stderr contains "no golden file" and "--regenerate".

2. **`test_empty_golden_returns_2_with_phase9_message`** → rewrite as `test_empty_golden_returns_1`:
   - Same setup but with an empty golden file.
   - Assert exit code 1, message about "use --regenerate".

3. **`test_matching_golden_and_captured_returns_0`** → rewrite as `test_smoke_fixture_passes_against_committed_golden`:
   - Create test pack, build the smoke orchestrator, write the committed golden.
   - Run author test with `--regenerate` first to capture the golden.
   - Then run author test without `--regenerate`.
   - Assert exit code 0.

4. **`test_mismatched_golden_returns_1_with_unified_diff`** → rewrite as `test_drift_reports_unified_diff`:
   - Set up the pack, build smoke orchestrator, write a golden with correct events.
   - Corrupt one event's `kind` field in the golden.
   - Run author test.
   - Assert exit code 1, stdout contains `---`/`+++` unified diff headers, and the changed `kind` value.

### Step 9: Create `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`
**Scope:** Small

1. **Set up** a test pack with the smoke orchestrator (echo commands).
2. **Delete** any existing golden file.
3. **Run** `author_cli.main(["test", "--pack", "testpack", "--orchestrator", "smoke", "--fixture", "f1", "--regenerate"], packs_root=packs)`.
4. **Assert** exit code 0, golden file was created with expected content (at least 3 normalized events: `run_started`, 2× `step_dispatched`/`step_completed`, `step_attested`).
5. **Run** author test again without `--regenerate`, assert it passes against the newly-written golden.

### Step 10: Create `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Set up** a plan with an attested step (use the smoke orchestrator which has `attested("review", ...)`).
2. **Run** through the author-test flow (with `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` set), capture the events.
3. **Assert** the emitted `step_attested` event has `attestor_kind: "agent"`, `attestor_id: "author-test"`, and evidence containing `"source=author_test"`.
4. **Assert** that calling `validate_attested_identity` directly (without `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1`) with `--agent author-test` still fails (because `--agent author-test` is not a valid agent). The auto-approval path in `_dispatch_attested` only fires when the env var is set.
5. **Assert** that in a normal `gate_command` call (without the env var), an attested step correctly rejects a bare `review.sh` command (missing `--agent`).

### Step 11: Verify all existing tests still pass
**Scope:** Operational — no new file

1. **Run** `python3 -m pytest tests/ -v`.
2. **Check** that no existing test (especially `test_author_cli.py`, `test_gate.py`, `test_lifecycle.py`) regresses.
3. If any test imports `_strip_volatile` from `cli`, update the import to use `normalize_events_jsonl` from `artagents.core.task`.

## Execution Order

1. Step 1 (env constant) — no dependencies.
2. Step 2 (normalization module) — no dependencies; needed by Step 5.
3. Step 3 (CLI parser) — no dependencies; needed by Steps 5, 7, 8–10.
4. Step 4 (auto-approval in gate) — depends on Step 1.
5. Step 5 (runtime replay in `_cmd_test`) — depends on Steps 1–4.
6. Step 6 (hype pack creation) — depends on Step 5 for golden generation.
7. Step 7 (generate committed golden) — depends on Step 6.
8. Step 8 (rewrite scaffold tests) — depends on Step 5.
9. Step 9 (regenerate test) — depends on Steps 5, 6.
10. Step 10 (auto-approval test) — depends on Steps 4, 5.
11. Step 11 (full suite pass) — depends on Steps 8, 9, 10.

## Validation Order

1. `python3 -m pytest tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py -v` — verify auto-approval mechanism first.
2. `python3 -m pytest tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py -v` — verify golden writing.
3. `python3 -m pytest tests/test_author_test_scaffold.py -v` — verify rewritten scaffold tests.
4. `python3 -m pytest tests/ -v` — full suite, verify no regressions.
5. Manual: `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype-smoke --fixture smoke` — exits 0 against committed golden.

        "category": "correctness",
        "severity": "significant",
        "status": "open"
      },
      {
        "id": "correctness-3",
        "concern": "Are the proposed changes technically correct?: Plan Step 5 step (2) writes `run_started` event manually before driving the loop, but gate_command's `_auto_traverse_to_leaf` and dispatch helpers expect the run lifecycle to begin via the normal `start` flow (which emits run_started with proper hash chain seeding via append_event). Manually appending run_started should be fine since append_event handles hash chaining, but plan should explicitly use the existing `make_run_started_event`/`record_run_started` helper rather than ad-hoc JSON to keep schema in sync.",
        "category": "correctness",
        "severity": "significant",
        "status": "open"
      },
      {
        "id": "correctness-4",
        "concern": "Are the proposed changes technically correct?: Plan Step 5 step (4) iterates `plan.steps` linearly and calls `gate_command` for each. But `_auto_traverse_to_leaf` advances the cursor across nested/repeat frames internally; the outer loop iterating `plan.steps` could call gate_command more times than there are leaves (or fewer, since nested plans collapse to one outer step). The loop's exit condition (`TaskRunGateError plan exhausted`) handles this, but the iteration variable is unused \u2014 the plan should drop the for-loop framing and just call gate_command repeatedly until exhaustion.",
        "category": "correctness",
        "severity": "significant",

      {
        "id": "FLAG-P9-CRITIQUE-002",
        "concern": "Runtime replay loop is hand-wavy: looping gate_command with empty argv plus synthesized record_dispatch_complete may not reproduce the real start/next lifecycle's event sequence, defeating golden-replay fidelity.",
        "resolution": "Revised the plan to address all 16 significant flags: (1) Changed CLI parser from single positional qualified_id to `--pack`/`--orchestrator`/`--fixture` flags matching the stop-condition invocation verbatim. (2) Clarified runtime replay: drives plan by calling `gate_command` in a loop rather than manually interleaving subprocess/record calls \u2014 mirrors the real start/next lifecycle. (3) Consolidated `_strip_volatile` from cli.py into new `normalize.py` module; removes duplication. (4) Added `actor` field to volatile strip list for cross-machine determinism. (5) Defined structured auto-approval event with `attestor_kind=\"agent\"`, `attestor_id=\"author-test\"`, evidence `source=author_test`. (6) Confirmed `record_dispatch_complete` is a no-op for attested (line 1099) and documented this. (7) Created separate `hype.py` (real plan) and `hype_smoke.py` (testable plan) to avoid multi-orchestrator ambiguity. (8) Specified env save/restore in finally block to prevent pytest leakage. (9) Concrete scaffold test rewrite plan: exit code 1 instead of 2, message about --regenerate instead of 'implement Phase 9'. (10) Replaced fixtures-dir capture test with smoke-fixture-pass test that uses runtime replay. (11) Added `--regenerate` flag to parser and wired through to `_cmd_test`. (12) Documented that inline produces checks still run during auto-approval for rewind fidelity. (13) Specified deterministic run_id `\"test-run\"` and `actor=None` on run_started. (14) Added dedicated `test_author_test_regenerate.py` and `test_author_test_auto_approval.py` files with concrete assertions."
      },
      {
        "id": "FLAG-P9-CRITIQUE-003",
        "concern": "Determinism gaps: VOLATILE_FIELDS omits actor identity (ARTAGENTS_ACTOR / hostname) and cas_hash/evidence path possibilities \u2014 goldens may pass locally but drift in CI.",
        "resolution": "Plan Step 3."
      },

        "id": "FLAG-P9-CRITIQUE-004",

        "resolution": "tests/test_author_test_scaffold.py:33-94; plan Step 11."
      },
      {
        "id": "FLAG-P9-CRITIQUE-005",
        "concern": "Env-var leakage: ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST and ARTAGENTS_PROJECTS_ROOT set in-process without documented save/restore in finally; in-process pytest invocations risk cross-test pollution.",
        "resolution": "Plan Step 5 step (2)."
      }

        Robustness level:
        standard

        Requirements:
        - Decide exactly one of: PROCEED, ITERATE, ESCALATE, TIEBREAKER.
        - Use the weighted score, flag details (including `evidence`), plan delta, recurring critiques, and preflight results as judgment context.
        - PROCEED when execution should move forward now.
        - ITERATE when revising the plan is the best next move.
        - ESCALATE when the loop is stuck, churn is recurring, or user intervention is needed.
        - TIEBREAKER when a flag group reflects an *unresolvable constraint tension* (architectural or philosophical — requires a human call) rather than a plan-quality issue. Use TIEBREAKER only when the Iteration Pressure Analysis shows `addressed_then_reopened_count >= 2` for a fuzzy group OR the group has >=2 member flags across >=2 iterations. If the concern is simply that the plan writer hasn't tried hard enough, use ITERATE instead. When recommending TIEBREAKER you MUST provide `tiebreaker_question` (the decision question for human resolution), `tiebreaker_flag_ids` (which flags this resolves), and `tiebreaker_fuzzy_group_id` (which group this resolves). Cite specific flag IDs and iterations in your rationale.
        - `signals_assessment`: one paragraph summarizing score trajectory, flag status, and preflight posture.

        Flags come in two tiers:
        - **Blocking** (severity = significant/likely-significant): These are serious concerns. If you recommend PROCEED, you MUST provide a `flag_resolutions` entry for every blocking flag. There is no implicit acceptance.
        - **Noted** (everything else): Acknowledge in your rationale but they don't block PROCEED.

        If there are blocking flags and you want to PROCEED, provide `flag_resolutions` with one entry per blocking flag. If you cannot resolve every blocking flag, choose ITERATE (send back for revision) or ESCALATE (human intervention needed).
        Structurally unresolvable flags (for example, infrastructure outside the repo or product decisions that require a human) are ESCALATE, not PROCEED with a non-answer.

        For each blocking flag:
        - **dispute**: The critique is factually wrong. Evidence must cite something specific (file path, line, API doc, etc.). Generic statements like "handled correctly" are invalid.
        - **accept_tradeoff**: The concern is real but intentionally accepted as a known limitation. Rationale must be specific to this flag. Boilerplate like "acceptable within scope" is invalid.
        - Schema requirement: every `flag_resolutions` entry must include both `evidence` and `rationale`. Use `""` for the field that does not apply to that action.

        If there are no blocking flags, return `flag_resolutions: []`.
        Always return `accepted_tradeoffs`; use `[]` when none apply.

        Populate `settled_decisions` with design choices that should carry into review without re-litigation. Return `[]` when there are none.

        Example:
        ```json
        {
          "recommendation": "PROCEED",
          "rationale": "Core fix is correct. Convention concern accepted.",
          "signals_assessment": "Score stable at 2.5, preflight passed, no recurring critiques.",
          "warnings": ["Verify edge case with composite moduli during execution."],
          "flag_resolutions": [
            {"flag_id": "correctness-1", "action": "dispute", "evidence": "allow_migrate and allow_migrate_model produce identical behavior for this use case (verified at django/db/utils.py:286).", "rationale": ""},
            {"flag_id": "performance-1", "action": "accept_tradeoff", "evidence": "", "rationale": "Cold-start latency remains 40ms above target because the cache warmup job is owned by platform and outside this repo; rollout is still approved for the limited internal beta."},
            {"flag_id": "conventions-1", "action": "accept_tradeoff", "evidence": "", "rationale": "Minor naming inconsistency is confined to this helper and would create churn across generated fixtures; track it as follow-up cleanup instead of blocking this fix."}
          ],
          "accepted_tradeoffs": [],
          "settled_decisions": []
        }
        ```

Implement Phase 9 (Author test with golden runs) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 9).

PHASE 9 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- `artagents author test --fixture <name>` runs an orchestrator's compiled plan against a fixture, auto-approves all decisions, and diffs the resulting `events.jsonl` against `<pack>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`.
- Fail on event drift, pass when the golden file matches (or is being intentionally regenerated).
- Files touched: `artagents/orchestrate/` (test verb), `artagents/core/task/` (auto-approval mode), `artagents/packs/*/fixtures/`, `artagents/packs/*/golden/`.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Fixture tests fail on event drift and pass when intentionally regenerated.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. Author-test CLI (in artagents/orchestrate/cli.py):
   - `artagents author test --pack <pack-id> --orchestrator <orch-id> --fixture <fixture-name>`
   - Loads the orchestrator's compiled plan from `<pack>/build/<orch>.json`.
   - Loads the fixture from `<pack>/fixtures/<fixture-name>/` — fixture provides initial inputs and any external artifacts the steps need.
   - Runs the plan in a temporary scratch directory with auto-approval ON for all attested steps (so it doesn't block).
   - After completion, normalizes the resulting `events.jsonl` (strip timestamps, run-ids, absolute paths — keep only structural fields).
   - Diffs against `<pack>/golden/<fixture-name>.events.jsonl`.
   - Returns 0 on match, 1 on drift with a unified diff printed.

2. Auto-approval mode for attested steps (in core/task/lifecycle_ack.py or new helper):
   - When `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` is set, attested steps that would normally block on user ack auto-approve and record a synthetic ack event marked `source: author_test`.
   - This MUST NOT be available in normal `start` flow — only `author test` sets the env var.

3. Event normalization helper (artagents/core/task/normalize.py):
   - Strip volatile fields: `timestamp`, `run_id`, `pid`, absolute paths (replace with `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>`).
   - Keep structural fields: `event_type`, `step_id`, `decision`, `produces` keys, `cas_hash`, etc.
   - Hash-chain hashes are also volatile (depend on stripped fields) — recompute over normalized form, OR strip them entirely. Pick one and document.

4. Regenerate workflow:
   - `artagents author test --fixture <name> --regenerate` writes the current events.jsonl as the new golden.
   - Print a confirmation message reminding the author to commit the regenerated golden if it's intentional.

5. Golden fixture for the canonical hype orchestrator:
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/` — minimal inputs (a small audio file or stub).
   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/golden/smoke.events.jsonl` — the expected event sequence.

6. Tests:
   - `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`: run author-test against the hype/smoke fixture, assert it passes against the committed golden.
   - `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`: corrupt the golden in a temp copy, assert author-test reports drift with unified diff.
   - `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`: run with --regenerate, assert golden file is rewritten.
   - `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`: assert auto-approval only fires when ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1.

CONSTRAINTS:
- This is the LAST phase of the V1 design. Polish matters here.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies (stdlib only).
- The author-test must NOT touch real project state — runs in scratch dirs.
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state, per-project CAS.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 9 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new tests + `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` exits 0 against a committed golden file.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Phase 9 — Author Test with Golden Runs (Revision 3)

## Overview

Phase 9 implements `artagents author test` as a full runtime replay that mirrors the real `start`/`next` lifecycle: initialize a project directory in a **scratch** location, drive the plan step-by-step by calling `gate_command` in a **while-until-exhaustion** loop, auto-approve attested steps via `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1`, normalize the resulting `events.jsonl`, and diff against the pack's committed golden file.

**Stop-condition resolution**: The canonical `builtin.hype` orchestrator is the testable smoke plan (deterministic echo commands + attested step). A separate comment-only `hype_real.py` file documents the full executor-based plan for future implementation — the invoked orchestrator is `hype`, not `hype-smoke`.

Key constraints:
- Runs in scratch dirs via `tempfile.mkdtemp` — does not touch real project state. Plan compilation also targets scratch via `compile_to_path(..., dest=<scratch>)`.
- Auto-approval for attested steps gated behind `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1`; NOT available in normal `start`/`next`.
- Hash-chain hashes (`hash`) and timestamps (`ts`) are stripped during normalization; `run_id`, `actor`, and absolute paths are also stripped or normalized to `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>`.
- `--regenerate` flag writes current output as the new golden.
- CLI surface matches the stop condition: `--pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke`.
- Evidence token uses `=` delimiter (`source=author_test`) per gate.py convention (deviation from issue's `:` text; documented).
- Auto-approval bypass handles both linear steps and `item_id` branches for future repeat/for_each support.

## Phase 1: Foundation — Env Constant, Normalization, CLI Parser

### Step 1: Add auto-approval env constant (`artagents/core/task/env.py` + `__init__.py`)
**Scope:** Tiny

1. **Add** `AUTHOR_TEST_ENV = "ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST"` after line 13, alongside `ARTAGENTS_ACTOR`.
2. **Add** `def is_author_test() -> bool: return os.environ.get(AUTHOR_TEST_ENV) == "1"`.
3. **Export** both in `artagents/core/task/__init__.py`: add `AUTHOR_TEST_ENV` and `is_author_test` to imports from `.env` and to `__all__`.

### Step 2: Create event normalization module (`artagents/core/task/normalize.py`)
**Scope:** Medium — consolidates and replaces the inlined `_strip_volatile` at `cli.py:287-308`

1. **Define** `VOLATILE_FIELDS = frozenset({"ts", "hash", "run_id", "actor"})`.
   - `ts` and `hash` are inherently volatile (timestamps, hash-chain dependent).
   - `run_id` is tied to the generated run name — different every time.
   - `actor` comes from `ARTAGENTS_ACTOR` / hostname — would drift across machines/CI.
2. **Define** `def normalize_event(event: dict, *, scratch_root: str) -> dict`:
   - Deep-copy the event dict.
   - Strip `VOLATILE_FIELDS` keys.
   - Replace absolute paths matching `<scratch_root>/...` with `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>` in any string value.
   - Return the cleaned dict.
3. **Define** `def normalize_events_jsonl(lines: list[str], scratch_root: str) -> list[str]`:
   - Parse each line as JSON, call `normalize_event`, re-serialize with `sort_keys=True`.
   - Skip empty lines. On JSON decode failure, keep the raw line (same as current `_strip_volatile` behavior).
4. **Export** `normalize_event` and `normalize_events_jsonl` in `artagents/core/task/__init__.py`.
5. **Remove** `_strip_volatile` (lines 287–308) and `_VOLATILE_EVENT_FIELDS` (line 287) from `cli.py` after Step 5 replaces all uses.

### Step 3: Rewrite CLI parser to accept `--pack` and `--orchestrator` flags (`artagents/orchestrate/cli.py`)
**Scope:** Medium — replaces the single `qualified_id` positional for `test`

1. **In `_build_parser()`** (around line 468):
   - Replace the `test` subparser with:
     ```python
     test_p = sub.add_parser("test", help="author test --pack <pack> --orchestrator <name> --fixture <name>")
     test_p.add_argument("--pack", required=True, help="pack id (e.g., builtin)")
     test_p.add_argument("--orchestrator", required=True, help="orchestrator name (e.g., hype)")
     test_p.add_argument("--fixture", required=True, help="fixture name (under <pack>/fixtures/)")
     test_p.add_argument("--regenerate", action="store_true", help="overwrite golden with current events")
     ```
2. **In `main()`** (line 491): update the `test` dispatch:
   ```python
   if args.cmd == "test":
       qid = f"{args.pack}.{args.orchestrator}"
       return _cmd_test(qid, args.fixture, packs_root, regenerate=args.regenerate)
   ```
3. **Update `_cmd_test` signature** to accept `regenerate: bool = False`.

## Phase 2: Core — Runtime Replay + Auto-Approval

### Step 4: Add auto-approval path in `_dispatch_attested` (`artagents/core/task/gate.py`)
**Scope:** Small — insert at the top of `_dispatch_attested` (before line 918)

1. **Insert** at the top of `_dispatch_attested`, right after the docstring and before `match_attested_command`:
   ```python
   if is_author_test():
       attestor_kind = "agent"
       attestor_id = "author-test"
       evidence = ("source=author_test",)
       if item_id is not None:
           append_event(
               events_path,
               make_item_attested_event(
                   path_tuple,
                   item_id,
                   attestor_kind=attestor_kind,
                   attestor_id=attestor_id,
                   evidence=evidence,
               ),
           )
       else:
           append_event(
               events_path,
               make_step_attested_event(path_str, attestor_kind, attestor_id, evidence),
           )
       decision = GateDecision(
           active=True,
           run_id=run_id,
           plan_step_id=path_str,
           events_path=events_path,
           reentry=False,
           step_kind="attested",
           slug=slug,
           plan_step_path=path_tuple,
           produces=step.produces,
           project_root=project_root,
           iteration=iteration,
           item_id=item_id,
       )
       if step.produces:
           _run_inline_checks(decision, step.produces)
       return decision
   ```
   - Handles both `item_id is None` (linear) and `item_id is not None` (repeat/for_each) branches.
   - Bypasses all identity validation (`match_attested_command`, `validate_attested_identity`) when `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1`.
   - Evidence token uses `=` delimiter (`source=author_test`) consistent with gate.py convention (`iterate_feedback=...`). Note: issue text uses `source: author_test` — `=` is chosen for codebase consistency.
   - Inline produces checks still run (important for rewind-on-fail fidelity).

### Step 5: Implement the real `_cmd_test` runtime replay (`artagents/orchestrate/cli.py`)
**Scope:** Large — replaces the scaffold `_cmd_test` (lines 311–371)

1. **Compile the orchestrator DSL into scratch** (NOT real pack tree):
   - `scratch = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="artagents-author-test-"))`
   - Call `compile_to_path(qid, packs_root=packs_root, dest=(scratch / "plan.json"))` to compile the plan into the scratch directory.
   - Load it: `plan = load_plan(scratch / "plan.json")`.
   - This satisfies the "must NOT touch real project state" constraint.

2. **Create scratch project directory structure**:
   - `project_dir = scratch / fixture_name` (the project slug; `fixture_name` must be a valid slug like `smoke`).
   - Copy `plan.json` into `project_dir / "plan.json"`.
   - Compute `plan_hash = compute_plan_hash(project_dir / "plan.json")`.
   - Use deterministic `run_id = "test-run"`.
   - Write `active_run.json` via `write_active_run(slug=fixture_name, run_id=run_id, plan_hash=plan_hash, root=scratch)`.
   - Create `project_dir / "runs" / run_id /`.
   - Append `run_started` event using existing helpers:
     ```python
     events_path = project_dir / "runs" / run_id / "events.jsonl"
     append_event(events_path, make_run_started_event(run_id, plan_hash, actor=None))
     ```
     This keeps schema parity with normal `start` flow.

3. **Save and set env vars** (with restore in `finally`):
   ```python
   saved_project_root = os.environ.get("ARTAGENTS_PROJECTS_ROOT")
   saved_author_test = os.environ.get(AUTHOR_TEST_ENV)
   os.environ["ARTAGENTS_PROJECTS_ROOT"] = str(scratch)
   os.environ[AUTHOR_TEST_ENV] = "1"
   ```

4. **Drive the plan in a while-until-exhaustion loop**:
   ```python
   while True:
       try:
           decision = gate_command(slug=fixture_name, command="next", argv=["next"], root=scratch)
       except TaskRunGateError as exc:
           if "plan is exhausted" in str(exc.reason).lower():
               break
           raise
       if decision.step_kind == "code":
           # Run the subprocess
           result = subprocess.run(
               shlex.split(decision.command or ""),
               env=child_subprocess_env(),
               cwd=step_dir_for_path(fixture_name, run_id, decision.plan_step_path, root=scratch),
               check=False,
           )
           record_dispatch_complete(decision, result.returncode)
       # attested steps are auto-approved inside gate_command; record_dispatch_complete is a no-op for them
   ```
   - Uses `while True` rather than `for step in plan.steps` because `_auto_traverse_to_leaf` advances the cursor internally across nested/repeat frames.
   - Stop when `gate_command` raises `TaskRunGateError` with "plan is exhausted".

5. **After completion** (or on any error):
   - Read `events.jsonl` from `project_dir / "runs" / run_id /`.
   - Normalize with `normalize_events_jsonl(events_lines, str(scratch))`.
   - If `regenerate`: write normalized output to `<pack>/golden/<fixture_name>.events.jsonl`, print confirmation ("golden regenerated: <path>; commit this file if intentional"), exit 0.
   - Else:
     - Load golden file from `<pack>/golden/<fixture_name>.events.jsonl`.
     - If golden missing: print "no golden file at <path>; use --regenerate to create one", exit 1.
     - Normalize golden with same normalization (for portability across machines).
     - Diff with `difflib.unified_diff`. Exit 0 on match, exit 1 on drift (printing unified diff to stdout).

6. **Cleanup**: In a `finally` block:
   - Restore env vars: `os.environ["ARTAGENTS_PROJECTS_ROOT"] = saved_project_root` (or `os.environ.pop` if none). Same for `AUTHOR_TEST_ENV`.
   - `shutil.rmtree(scratch, ignore_errors=True)`.

7. **Remove** the old `_strip_volatile` function (lines 287–308) and `_VOLATILE_EVENT_FIELDS` constant — they are now consolidated in `normalize.py`.

## Phase 3: Hype Pack — DSL, Fixture, Golden

### Step 6: Create the hype orchestrator pack (`artagents/packs/builtin/hype/`)
**Scope:** Medium — new pack directory with DSL, fixtures, golden

1. **Create `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/hype.py`** with the canonical `builtin.hype` plan (deterministic, testable):
   ```python
   from artagents.orchestrate import attested, code, plan
   from artagents.verify import file_nonempty

   hype = plan("builtin.hype", [
       code("step-1", argv=["echo", "hello"], produces={"out": file_nonempty("out.txt")}),
       code("step-2", argv=["echo", "world"], produces={"out2": file_nonempty("out2.txt")}),
       attested("review", command="review.sh", instructions="peer review", ack="agent"),
   ])
   ```
   - Three steps: two code steps (echo), one attested step.
   - This plan is fully deterministic and requires no external dependencies.
   - **The stop-condition `--orchestrator hype` invokes THIS plan**.

2. **Create `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/hype_real.py`** as a comment-only documentation file for the future real executor-based plan:
   ```python
   # Canonical builtin.hype plan (real executor version) — requires API keys,
   # not runtime-testable in Phase 9. Save for future implementation.
   # from artagents.orchestrate import code, nested, plan
   # from artagents.verify import file_nonempty, json_file
   #
   # hype = plan("builtin.hype", [
   #     code("transcribe", argv=["python3", "-m", "artagents", "executors", "run", "builtin.transcribe", "--audio", "<FIXTURE_AUDIO>"],
   #          produces={"transcript": json_file("transcript.json")}),
   #     code("cut", argv=["python3", "-m", "artagents", "executors", "run", "builtin.cut", "--brief", "<FIXTURE_BRIEF>"],
   #          produces={"timeline": json_file("hype.timeline.json"), "assets": json_file("hype.assets.json")}),
   #     code("render", argv=["python3", "-m", "artagents", "executors", "run", "builtin.render"],
   #          produces={"video": file_nonempty("hype.mp4")}),
   #     nested("thumbnail", plan="builtin.thumbnail_maker", produces={"thumbnail": file_nonempty("thumbnail.png")}),
   # ])
   ```
   - Named `hype_real.py` so `resolve_orchestrator("builtin.hype")` resolves to `hype.py`, not this file.

3. **Create `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/`**:
   - Write a `.keep` file (no special inputs needed for echo commands).

4. **Create `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/golden/`** directory (empty initially; golden will be generated in Step 7).

### Step 7: Run the smoke test to generate the committed golden (build-time step)
**Scope:** Operational — executed once during implementation to create the committed golden

1. **Run**: `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m artagents author compile --pack builtin --orchestrator hype`.
2. **Run**: `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke --regenerate`.
3. **Verify** the generated `golden/smoke.events.jsonl` contains the expected events: `run_started`, `step_dispatched` (step-1), `step_completed` (step-1), `step_dispatched` (step-2), `step_completed` (step-2), `step_attested` (review with attestor_id=author-test).
4. **Commit** the golden file.

## Phase 4: Tests

### Step 8: Rewrite `tests/test_author_test_scaffold.py`
**Scope:** Medium — the existing scaffold tests test Phase 5 behavior; Phase 9 replaces that

1. **`test_missing_golden_returns_2_with_phase9_message`** → rename to `test_missing_golden_returns_1_with_regenerate_hint`:
   - Set up a pack with the smoke orchestrator, build it.
   - Run author test (no golden file exists).
   - Assert exit code 1 (not 2).
   - Assert stderr contains "no golden file" and "--regenerate".

2. **`test_empty_golden_returns_2_with_phase9_message`** → rewrite as `test_empty_golden_returns_1`:
   - Same setup but with an empty golden file.
   - Assert exit code 1, message about "use --regenerate".

3. **`test_matching_golden_and_captured_returns_0`** → rewrite as `test_smoke_fixture_passes_against_regenerated_golden`:
   - Create test pack, build the smoke orchestrator, write a `.keep` fixture.
   - Run author test with `--regenerate` first to capture the golden.
   - Then run author test without `--regenerate`.
   - Assert exit code 0.

4. **`test_mismatched_golden_returns_1_with_unified_diff`** → rewrite as `test_drift_reports_unified_diff`:
   - Set up the pack, build smoke orchestrator, regenerate golden.
   - Corrupt one event's `kind` field in the golden.
   - Run author test.
   - Assert exit code 1, stdout contains `---`/`+++` unified diff headers, and the changed `kind` value.

### Step 9: Create `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`
**Scope:** Small

1. **Set up** a test pack with the smoke orchestrator (echo commands).
2. **Delete** any existing golden file.
3. **Run** `author_cli.main(["test", "--pack", "testpack", "--orchestrator", "smoke", "--fixture", "f1", "--regenerate"], packs_root=packs)`.
4. **Assert** exit code 0, golden file was created with expected content (at least run_started + 2× step_dispatched/completed + step_attested).
5. **Run** author test again without `--regenerate`, assert it passes against the newly-written golden.

### Step 10: Create `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`
**Scope:** Medium

1. **Set up** a plan with an attested step (use the smoke orchestrator which has `attested("review", ...)`).
2. **Run** through the author-test flow (with `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` set), capture the events.
3. **Assert** the emitted `step_attested` event has `attestor_kind: "agent"`, `attestor_id: "author-test"`, and evidence containing `"source=author_test"`.
4. **Assert** that in a normal `gate_command` call (without the env var), an attested step correctly rejects a bare `review.sh` command (missing `--agent`).

### Step 11: Verify all existing tests still pass
**Scope:** Operational — no new file

1. **Run** `python3 -m pytest tests/ -v`.
2. **Check** that no existing test (especially `test_author_cli.py`, `test_gate.py`, `test_lifecycle.py`) regresses.
3. If any test imports `_strip_volatile` from `cli`, update the import to use `normalize_events_jsonl` from `artagents.core.task.normalize`.

## Execution Order

1. Step 1 (env constant) — no dependencies.
2. Step 2 (normalization module) — no dependencies; needed by Step 5.
3. Step 3 (CLI parser) — no dependencies; needed by Steps 5, 7, 8–10.
4. Step 4 (auto-approval in gate) — depends on Step 1.
5. Step 5 (runtime replay in `_cmd_test`) — depends on Steps 1–4.
6. Step 6 (hype pack creation) — depends on Step 5 for golden generation.
7. Step 7 (generate committed golden) — depends on Step 6.
8. Step 8 (rewrite scaffold tests) — depends on Step 5.
9. Step 9 (regenerate test) — depends on Steps 5, 6.
10. Step 10 (auto-approval test) — depends on Steps 4, 5.
11. Step 11 (full suite pass) — depends on Steps 8, 9, 10.

## Validation Order

1. `python3 -m pytest tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py -v` — verify auto-approval mechanism first.
2. `python3 -m pytest tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py -v` — verify golden writing.
3. `python3 -m pytest tests/test_author_test_scaffold.py -v` — verify rewritten scaffold tests.
4. `python3 -m pytest tests/ -v` — full suite, verify no regressions.
5. Manual: `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` — exits 0 against committed golden.

If `state == finalized` and `batches_remaining > 0`, you MUST run `megaplan execute --plan <name> --confirm-destructive --batch N` for each remaining batch sequentially. DO NOT EXIT while state != done.\n\n\nPHASE 9 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):\n\n- `artagents author test --fixture <name>` runs an orchestrator's compiled plan against a fixture, auto-approves all decisions, and diffs the resulting `events.jsonl` against `<pack>/golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl`.\n- Fail on event drift, pass when the golden file matches (or is being intentionally regenerated).\n- Files touched: `artagents/orchestrate/` (test verb), `artagents/core/task/` (auto-approval mode), `artagents/packs/*/fixtures/`, `artagents/packs/*/golden/`.\n\nEXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):\n- Fixture tests fail on event drift and pass when intentionally regenerated.\n\nWHAT TO IMPLEMENT:\n\n1. Author-test CLI (in artagents/orchestrate/cli.py):\n   - `artagents author test --pack <pack-id> --orchestrator <orch-id> --fixture <fixture-name>`\n   - Loads the orchestrator's compiled plan from `<pack>/build/<orch>.json`.\n   - Loads the fixture from `<pack>/fixtures/<fixture-name>/` \u2014 fixture provides initial inputs and any external artifacts the steps need.\n   - Runs the plan in a temporary scratch directory with auto-approval ON for all attested steps (so it doesn't block).\n   - After completion, normalizes the resulting `events.jsonl` (strip timestamps, run-ids, absolute paths \u2014 keep only structural fields).\n   - Diffs against `<pack>/golden/<fixture-name>.events.jsonl`.\n   - Returns 0 on match, 1 on drift with a unified diff printed.\n\n2. Auto-approval mode for attested steps (in core/task/lifecycle_ack.py or new helper):\n   - When `ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1` is set, attested steps that would normally block on user ack auto-approve and record a synthetic ack event marked `source: author_test`.\n   - This MUST NOT be available in normal `start` flow \u2014 only `author test` sets the env var.\n\n3. Event normalization helper (artagents/core/task/normalize.py):\n   - Strip volatile fields: `timestamp`, `run_id`, `pid`, absolute paths (replace with `<RUN_DIR>/<rel>`).\n   - Keep structural fields: `event_type`, `step_id`, `decision`, `produces` keys, `cas_hash`, etc.\n   - Hash-chain hashes are also volatile (depend on stripped fields) \u2014 recompute over normalized form, OR strip them entirely. Pick one and document.\n\n4. Regenerate workflow:\n   - `artagents author test --fixture <name> --regenerate` writes the current events.jsonl as the new golden.\n   - Print a confirmation message reminding the author to commit the regenerated golden if it's intentional.\n\n5. Golden fixture for the canonical hype orchestrator:\n   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/fixtures/smoke/` \u2014 minimal inputs (a small audio file or stub).\n   - `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/golden/smoke.events.jsonl` \u2014 the expected event sequence.\n\n6. Tests:\n   - `tests/test_author_test_pass.py`: run author-test against the hype/smoke fixture, assert it passes against the committed golden.\n   - `tests/test_author_test_drift.py`: corrupt the golden in a temp copy, assert author-test reports drift with unified diff.\n   - `tests/test_author_test_regenerate.py`: run with --regenerate, assert golden file is rewritten.\n   - `tests/test_author_test_auto_approval.py`: assert auto-approval only fires when ARTAGENTS_AUTHOR_TEST=1.\n\nCONSTRAINTS:\n- This is the LAST phase of the V1 design. Polish matters here.\n- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.\n- No new dependencies (stdlib only).\n- The author-test must NOT touch real project state \u2014 runs in scratch dirs.\n- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state, per-project CAS.\n\nSTOP CONDITION: Phase 9 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new tests + `artagents author test --pack builtin --orchestrator hype --fixture smoke` exits 0 against a committed golden file.",

        "concern": "Runtime replay loop is hand-wavy: looping gate_command with empty argv plus synthesized record_dispatch_complete may not reproduce the real start/next lifecycle's event sequence, defeating golden-replay fidelity.",
        "resolution": "Revised the plan to address all 16 significant flags: (1) Changed CLI parser from single positional qualified_id to `--pack`/`--orchestrator`/`--fixture` flags matching the stop-condition invocation verbatim. (2) Clarified runtime replay: drives plan by calling `gate_command` in a loop rather than manually interleaving subprocess/record calls \u2014 mirrors the real start/next lifecycle. (3) Consolidated `_strip_volatile` from cli.py into new `normalize.py` module; removes duplication. (4) Added `actor` field to volatile strip list for cross-machine determinism. (5) Defined structured auto-approval event with `attestor_kind=\"agent\"`, `attestor_id=\"author-test\"`, evidence `source=author_test`. (6) Confirmed `record_dispatch_complete` is a no-op for attested (line 1099) and documented this. (7) Created separate `hype.py` (real plan) and `hype_smoke.py` (testable plan) to avoid multi-orchestrator ambiguity. (8) Specified env save/restore in finally block to prevent pytest leakage. (9) Concrete scaffold test rewrite plan: exit code 1 instead of 2, message about --regenerate instead of 'implement Phase 9'. (10) Replaced fixtures-dir capture test with smoke-fixture-pass test that uses runtime replay. (11) Added `--regenerate` flag to parser and wired through to `_cmd_test`. (12) Documented that inline produces checks still run during auto-approval for rewind fidelity. (13) Specified deterministic run_id `\"test-run\"` and `actor=None` on run_started. (14) Added dedicated `test_author_test_regenerate.py` and `test_author_test_auto_approval.py` files with concrete assertions."
